The average revenue per user (ARPU) for mobile applications can vary depending on various factors such as the type of app, its features, and the market conditions. Some common metrics used to measure ARPU include:

1. Average Revenue Per User (ARPU): This metric measures how much money an app generates from users' purchases or usage.
2. Conversion Rate: The percentage of users who convert their purchase into actual use by clicking through to the app's website or performing other actions.
3. Acquisition Cost: The cost associated with acquiring new users to the app, including costs related to advertising, promotions, and other marketing activities.
4. Return On Investment (ROI): The ratio of revenue generated to the total investment made in the app.

It's important to note that these metrics may not be directly comparable across different types of apps or markets. Additionally, the ARPU can fluctuate based on changes in user behavior and engagement with the app.
